# Skills · T · part 24

838 entries, tufte-chart to Twitter Command Center (Search + Post). [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **tufte-chart**: Render a real, publication-ready data graphic (SVG or HTML) that obeys Tufte's principles — minimal ink, range-frame axes, direct labels, honest proportions. Use when someone wants to design, build, create, draw, plot, or visualize a chart or graph the Tufte way, or to rebuild a chart after a critique found problems. This is the skill that outputs an actual chart file. Not for critiquing an existing graphic (`tufte-critique`). ([gnurio/tufte-vdqi-plugin](https://github.com/gnurio/tufte-vdqi-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/tufte-chart) · ★306)
- **tufte-critique**: Critique a data graphic against a nine-criterion rubric derived from Tufte's VDQI — score it, name the chartjunk species present, compute the lie factor, compare against the book's named-failure catalogue, and return prioritised fixes tagged with the Tufte remedy (B1–B7), genre to switch to (C1–C10), and exemplar to emulate. Use when someone asks whether a chart, graph, plot, or dashboard is any good; what is wrong with it; whether it is misleading or distorted; or how to clean up, declutter, or simplify it. Not for producing a chart file (`tufte-chart`). ([gnurio/tufte-vdqi-plugin](https://github.com/gnurio/tufte-vdqi-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/tufte-critique) · ★306)
- **tufte-data-viz**: Make data visualizations potent in the tradition of Edward Tufte's "The Visual Display of Quantitative Information." Use BEFORE building ANY chart, graph, plot, table, diagram, dashboard, coverage bar, stat callout, or node-link graphic — in HTML/SVG, a plotting library, or an artifact. Triggers: "chart", "graph", "plot", "visualization", "dashboard", "make it potent / Tufte-style", "data-ink", "small multiples", "sparkline", "bipartite", "coverage bars", or any request to show quantities visually. ([sandbornm/my-claude-skills](https://github.com/sandbornm/my-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/tufte-data-viz) · ★18)

- **tvs-architect**: 当用户要求架构评审、设计判断、复杂 bug 根因分析、技术方案比较、长期可维护性建议，或说"这问题查不出来、帮我定位根因、两个方案选哪个"时使用。作用：基于真实代码证据做架构分析、根因诊断与方案取舍评估。 ([inksnowhailong/ai-tools-skills](https://github.com/inksnowhailong/ai-tools-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tvs-architect) · ★2)

- **tvs-deep-interview**: 当用户说"深度访谈、先问我、帮我梳理、不要假设、帮我想清楚、我想做X但说不太清、这个需求怎么提才对"，或任何需求/提问模糊、直接动手容易做错方向时使用。作用：思维教练式访谈——先分诊需求量级（轻/中/重），用单问题追问帮用户收敛想法，用扩展卡帮用户想到没想到的盲区，产出与量级匹配的规格；执行前必须经用户显式批准。日常小需求也适用，不是只服务大项目。 ([inksnowhailong/ai-tools-skills](https://github.com/inksnowhailong/ai-tools-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/tvs-deep-interview) · ★2)
